=== RUN   TestHTTPAPICommandConstructionHasNoRuntimeSideEffects
Serve the versioned private operator API on loopback or an explicitly allowed
VPN address. Reads need no application login after the private peer and TLS
boundary is validated. The service has no order, LIVE, gate, kill-switch, engine
start/stop, or activation-manifest route and never starts the engine.

All controls are server-owned. Read routes accept no query or request body, and
the daemon provides no login form, symbol field, numeric field, reason field, or
typed confirmation UI.

Usage:
  httpapi [flags]

Flags:
      --allowed-cidr strings           Private client CIDR accepted by the daemon; repeat for multiple networks (default [127.0.0.0/8])
      --bind string                    Loopback or private/VPN bind IP literal (default "127.0.0.1")
      --capability-public-key string   Ed25519 public-key file enabling signed one-time optimization mutations
      --port int                       Private HTTPS port (default 37086)
      --public-url string              Canonical private HTTPS origin, including effective port (default "https://127.0.0.1:37086")
      --security-db string             Separate 0600 nonce, idempotency and mutation-audit database
      --tls-cert string                PEM TLS certificate for direct TLS termination
      --tls-forwarded                  Accept TLS termination only from --trusted-proxy
      --tls-key string                 PEM TLS private key for direct TLS termination
      --trusted-proxy string           Exact private proxy IP allowed to supply one-hop forwarded identity
--- PASS: TestHTTPAPICommandConstructionHasNoRuntimeSideEffects (0.00s)
